Want to upload a video or describe the footage so I can start drafting it? **────────────** # Writing Action Ideas --- Title: Writing Action Ideas Conversation ID: 67e889b7-6164-8007-b52e-fcb5a17f99d0 Created: 2025-03-30 00:00:55 UTC Last Updated: 2025-03-30 01:39:41 UTC Model: Unknown Model --- > **User (2025-03-30 00:00:55 UTC):** > > List actions useful for writing **Assistant (2025-03-30 00:00:55 UTC):** Here are some **useful actions for writing** that you can integrate using OpenAPI custom actions in OpenAI or build yourself: --- ### 1. **Grammar and Spelling Check** - **Description:** Sends text to an API that returns grammar, punctuation, and spelling corrections. - **Useful API:** [LanguageTool API](https://languagetool.org/http-api/swagger-ui/#/) - **Action:** `checkGrammar` --- ### 2. **Text Summarization** - **Description:** Summarizes longer text into concise points or paragraphs. - **Useful API:** OpenAI API with `gpt-3.5-turbo` or `gpt-4`, or other NLP services like HuggingFace. - **Action:** `summarizeText` --- ### 3. **Tone and Style Adjustment** - **Description:** Changes the tone (e.g., professional, casual, friendly) or writing style (e.g., persuasive, academic). - **Useful API:** OpenAI API with a prompt tuned for tone/style. - **Action:** `adjustTone` --- ### 4. **Content Idea Generation** - **Description:** Generates blog post ideas, outlines, or topic suggestions based on a keyword or niche. - **Useful API:** OpenAI or GPT-powered services. - **Action:** `generateContentIdeas` --- ### 5. **Plagiarism Detection** - **Description:** Checks if submitted content is plagiarized or duplicated. - **Useful API:** Copyscape, PlagScan, Grammarly Business APIs (requires approval). - **Action:** `checkPlagiarism` --- ### 6. **Headline or Title Generator** - **Description:** Suggests catchy headlines for articles or social media posts. - **Action:** `generateHeadline` --- ### 7. **Keyword Optimization (SEO)** - **Description:** Suggests or analyzes SEO keywords within content. - **Useful API:** Semrush, Ahrefs, or other SEO platforms (some offer APIs). - **Action:** `optimizeKeywords` --- ### 8. **Citation Formatter** - **Description:** Converts source data into formatted citations (APA, MLA, Chicago). - **Useful API:** [CiteThisForMe](https://www.citethisforme.com/) (no public API, but alternatives exist). - **Action:** `formatCitation` --- ### 9. **Content Translation** - **Description:** Translates written content into another language. - Should I let him see or smell her body so he knows she's gone?

While their understanding of **death** isn’t like ours, they *do* understand **loss**, **absence**, and **change**.

Behavioral changes in surviving animals—withdrawal, searching, vocalizing, loss of appetite—are all documented responses to death.

The Emotional Lives of Animals* - *AVMA Pet Loss and Bereavement Guidelines* --- ### 2. **Should the Kitten See or Smell the Mother's Body?** **Yes**, if possible, **let him see or smell her body**—but do it gently and briefly.

Some cats will approach, sniff, sit with the body.

No bright lights or commotion. - After a few minutes, remove the body and gently comfort the kitten. **Why this works:** In the wild and in multi-pet households, animals often approach deceased companions and then visibly change behavior—often showing resignation or a shift into mourning.
